# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-11-03)

## Corpus Check
- 4923 files · ~10,993,865 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 114 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 34, .css 17, .entitlements 11)

## Summary
- 67085 nodes · 192063 edges · 1402 communities (1276 shown, 126 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 8030 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `4ec9e6c4`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
